# Yume Aesthetics — LinkedIn Profile

## About

At Yume Aesthetics, we believe that healthy skin starts with understanding what your skin is reacting to. Located in Burwood, Sydney, our clinic is dedicated to helping our clients feel less overwhelmed by their skin while feeling educated, supported, and confident. We offer personalized skin journeys focused on long-term skin health and real results, utilizing advanced technologies like the Stellar M22 XPL and LHALA Peel.

Our comprehensive services include barrier repair, targeted acne and pigmentation treatments, custom facials, and professional eyelash extensions. Every treatment plan is tailored to your unique concerns and goals, beginning with an in-depth consultation and skin analysis. No trends. No assumptions. Just a strategy tailored to your skin.

Skin is complex… but it doesn’t have to be for you.

Take it from me — I learnt the hard way.
I trialled and tested every product out there, thinking it would be the solution for my skin… only to realise I was actually damaging my skin barrier.

After seeking professional advice and introducing a combination of targeted skin treatments, the right homecare, and a few changes to my lifestyle and diet, I started seeing a drastic difference in the health of my skin.

Now, I want to help you experience the same — because I know the confidence that glowing, healthy skin can bring. 🤍

If you’re ready to stop guessing and start seeing results, DM me the word GLOW, and let’s start your skin journey together. ✨

**Caption**:
This is what one session of M22 XPL can do ✨

After just one treatment, we can already see visible improvement in post-inflammatory erythema (PIE), overall redness and active acne breakouts.

M22 XPL is designed to target vascular redness, post-acne marks and inflammation while supporting a clearer, more even-looking complexion over time.

Results will vary depending on your skin, concern and treatment plan, which is why every journey at Yume Aesthetics begins with an in-depth skin consultation and skin analysis.

Ready to start your skin journey? DM us “XPL” or book your consultation via the link in our bio 🤍

**Caption**:
Breakouts don’t always come from one cause — acne is usually triggered by a combination of factors.

Hormonal changes, stress, barrier damage, over-exfoliating, using the wrong products, diet, and lack of consistency can all contribute to ongoing breakouts.
When the cause is identified properly, treatment becomes much more effective and results last longer.

Healthy skin starts with understanding what your skin is reacting to. At Yume Aesthetics, your skin journey starts with an in-depth consultation where we take the time to understand your skin concerns, history, lifestyle, and goals, allowing us to create a customised treatment plan tailored to you.

Book in your consultation at Yume Aesthetics to find the triggers behind your breakouts.

At Yume Aesthetics, our skin analysis is designed to understand what is happening beneath the surface so we can create a treatment plan that actually works for your skin.

What we assess:
Hydration levels, congestion, pigmentation, redness, oil balance and overall skin function
The type and cause of concerns like acne, sensitivity and uneven texture
How your skin is likely to respond to treatments and products

This allows us to build a personalised plan focused on long term skin health and real results.

No trends. No assumptions. Just a strategy tailored to your skin.

Book your consultation at Yume Aesthetics to get started.

#burwoodskinclinic #skinanalysis #sydneyskincare #aestheticssydney #sydneyskinclinic

**Caption**:
A damaged skin barrier is one of the most common reasons for breakouts, sensitivity, redness, and skin that just won’t improve no matter what you use.

When the barrier is compromised, your skin loses moisture more easily and becomes more reactive to products, treatments, and environmental factors.

This is why repairing the barrier is often the first step before treating concerns like acne, pigmentation, or texture.

Book a consultation at Yume Aesthetics to find out if your skin barrier needs repair before starting your next treatment plan.

#burwoodskinclinic #skinbarrier #sydneyskincare #skinhealth #aestheticssydney

**Caption**:
Sunscreen isn’t only for sunny days.

UV exposure happens even when it’s cloudy, cold, or when you’re indoors near windows.
Wearing SPF only when the sun is out leaves your skin unprotected most of the time — which can lead to pigmentation, premature ageing, and slower skin healing.

Consistent daily SPF is one of the most important steps in any skincare routine, especially if you’re having treatments or working on skin concerns.

Protecting your skin every day helps maintain your results and keeps your skin healthy long term.

Book a consultation at Yume Aesthetics to make sure your skincare routine is supporting your skin, not working against it.

#burwoodskinclinic #spf #sydneyskincare #skinhealth #aestheticssydney

- **Video**: N/A

**Caption**:
Night time is repair time.

While you sleep, your skin shifts into regeneration mode — making your PM routine one of the most important steps in achieving healthy, resilient skin.

At Yume Aesthetics, our recommended evening routine featuring the Genabelle PDRN line focuses on deep hydration, barrier support and skin renewal.

Step 1: Pink Cloud Foam Cleanser
A gentle cleanse that removes SPF, makeup and impurities without disrupting the skin barrier.

Step 2: PDRN Rejuvenating Toner
Lightweight hydration that helps prep the skin and support recovery.

Step 3: PDRN Hyperboost 3% Ampoule
A concentrated formula designed to support skin regeneration, improve texture and promote a brighter, more even-looking complexion.

Step 4: PDRN Rejuvenating Cream
Locks in moisture and strengthens the barrier so skin feels calm, plump and supported by morning.

This routine is particularly beneficial for acne-prone, compromised, post-treatment or dehydrated skin needing extra support.

Your PM routine should be tailored to your skin — not trends. If you’re unsure what your skin truly needs at night, book a consultation at Yume Aesthetics and let us create a personalised plan for you.

#burwoodskinclinic #sydneyskincare #pdrnskincare #customskincare #aestheticssydney

Ever feel like your skincare just isn’t doing anything anymore?

Sometimes it’s not your products — it’s your skin needing a reset.

When dead skin builds up and cell turnover slows down, even the best serums can struggle to penetrate properly. The LHALA Peel is designed to gently resurface the skin, support healthy cell renewal and improve overall texture and brightness without aggressively stripping the barrier.

It’s ideal for skin that feels dull, congested or “stuck,” and for those wanting visible refinement while still prioritising long-term skin health.

At Yume Aesthetics, every LHALA Peel is selected following a professional consultation to ensure it’s right for your skin type and goals.

If your routine isn’t hitting the way it used to, it may be time for a professional reset.

Meet the face behind Yume Aesthetics ✨

Hi, I’m Emily, a Dermal and Beauty Therapist 💆🏻‍♀️

My passion for skincare started years ago when I was struggling to find a solution for my own skin. I know firsthand how overwhelming and frustrating it can feel, but also how empowering it is when your skin finally starts to feel healthy and confident again. 🤍

With over 4 years of experience in the beauty industry, my goal is to simplify the confusing world of skincare and help you understand your skin. Every face is different, and I love creating tailored, results-driven plans that actually make sense, and make a difference.

If you’ve been wanting to feel confident in your skin again, I’d love to help you get there. 💫

Ready to start your skin journey? Book in a consultation today! 💌

**Caption**:
Want to learn how to create the DAINTIEST of dainty lashes?? Say no more I got youu💁🏻‍♀️

My client here has very sparse thin natural lashes and so we usually do a wispy luxe set even though she prefers the wispy lite look. 

Some tips for creating the DAINTIEST lash set: 

1. When choosing lash diameter for spikes in wispy sets, I’ve found that thin 0.07 spikes produce the daintiest and most spiky look. 

2. Dainty sets are usually more airy as well so I fill the set up with around 50-60% spikes 

3. Drop the volume lengths down by 2-3mm to further enhance the airiness of the set

Would love to know if you guys have any other tips or if you found this helpful!

𝑳𝑨𝑺𝑯 𝑻𝑹𝑨𝑵𝑺𝑭𝑶𝑹𝑴𝑨𝑻𝑰𝑶𝑵 ⋆｡°✩

My client requested a natural/light uniform set and so we had originally opted for a hybrid set. BUTT we ended up doing a ‘modified’ wispy lite set with same length spikes and base so that the spikes would stand out a little more (as opposed to classics). Although the same lengths were used, the spikes were still noticeable as they were placed on the top/middle layers- I lovee how subtle they are!! We also decided to do more spikes than volume to maintain the airy and natural look.

**Caption**:
Ever wonder what goes on during your lash appointment? 

As a handmade lash artist, we are hand-making our fans on the spot during your appointment. This is why sets can sometimes take up to 3 hours! Here are some benefits of handmade fans: 

CUSTOMISATION 👩🏻‍🎨
It makes it a whole lot easier to customise a set with because you can control how narrow/ wide you want your fan. You can also control the amount you’re picking up which is based on the desired volume of the set you want to achieve AND the client’s natural lash health.

BETTER RETENTION 💁🏻‍♀️
Handmaking fans allows for a loose base which means you can wrap a fan around the natural lash and basically engulf it so you know she won’t budge.

SUPER LIGHTWEIGHT AND FLUFFY ☁️
Since theres no additional weight from excess glue you best believe these are the lightest and fluffiest that lash extensions can get. 

Using @shop.yumeaesthetics 0.03 Dream Collection Lash Trays ☁️ LITERALLY FANNING LIKE BUTTERRR (coming soon hehe)

𝗅𝖺𝗌𝗁𝖾𝗌 𝖼𝗎𝗌𝗍𝗈𝗆𝗂𝗌𝖾𝖽 𝗍𝗈 𝘺𝘰𝘶 ⊹₊｡ꕤ˚₊⊹

Having a lash artist that can customise a set is sooo important because each and every eye is different and this can sometimes include differences in our own two eyes. In this set I increased the lengths on one of the eyes just a little more than the other to balance them out as my client noticed that one side would always sit higher than the other despite a symmetrical mapping. Can you tell which eye it is?👀

**Caption**:
𝗍𝗁𝗂𝗌 𝗂𝗌 𝗒𝗈𝗎𝗋 𝗌𝗂𝗀𝗇 𝗍𝗈 𝗖𝗛𝗔𝗦𝗘 𝗬𝗢𝗨𝗥 𝗗𝗥𝗘𝗔𝗠𝗦 ⋆˚⟡˖ ࣪

This isn’t my typical post and I’m gonna be super vulnerable with you all so please bare with me🥹 As most of my clients may know I started lashing as a side hustle during uni because I wasn’t enjoying what I was studying at the time. Before that, I was constantly having breakdowns, feeling unfulfilled, and questioning what I wanted to do with my life. Even during my first & second year of lashing, I knew that I loved it but was too scared of going all in. As a result, I couldn’t give my business as much time and effort as I would’ve liked, leaving me still feeling unfulfilled. Fast forward to mid 2023, I finally decided to take action and choose happiness, despite what others thought of me. Its been an amazing year of growth and discovery and I am truly so so grateful that I am able to do what I do for a living. I feel so much more fulfilled and even excited to work- sometimes too excited I am literally a workaholic now🤣 Anyways I just wanted to share my story to bring some comfort for those that are feeling the same way that I was, know that you are not alone and that I am just a DM away. This is your sign to take control of your happiness! 

My 3 Day Lash Fundamentals Course will teach you everything you need to know about lashing & business so that you can start your new journey with confidence. If you’re interested in training with me, send me a DM for more information 💌

Lash extensions should always be customised & tailored to the client’s eye shape/ features while taking into account their preferences. My client requested for a set that would open up her eyes and make them sparkle✨We opted for a wet lash set for a more spiky look that wouldn’t be too overpowering & doll eye shape to really open up her eyes. Ever had a client ask you to do a natural wet look? 

The solution to this is thin spikes! You can basically mimic the look of classics but with thin volume spikes. The reason why I actually love this over the classics is because you can wrap the spikes around the natural lash, therefore better retention🙌🏻 Think classics but better!! 

My client here has actually tried every lash set on my menu before and safe to say that this set is THE ONE, especially because she’s been trying to have more makeup-free days. 

𝗦𝗘𝗧 𝗗𝗘𝗧𝗔𝗜𝗟𝗦:
Wet lash 
10-14mm
CC curl 
Cat-eye

𝘁𝗵𝗲 𝗗𝗥𝗘𝗔𝗠𝗜𝗘𝗦𝗧 𝘄𝗲𝘁 𝗹𝗮𝘀𝗵 𝘀𝗲𝘁☁️

Been loving the wet look lately and clearly so have you guys (literally all my hybrid regulars have converted to wet lash & I’m not complaining😩). Here are some tips on how I create that perfect wet lash look: 

1. DIRECTION is so so important! Make sure the lashes are placed perpendicular to the lash line

2. Ensure each closed fan is the same thickness for consistency

3. Use 0.07 lashes for a spikier & more defined look

Like, save and share this vid if these tips helped 💌

Another lash breakdown for yall since u guys seemed to enjoy the last one😌

𝑪𝒍𝒊𝒆𝒏𝒕 𝒓𝒆𝒒𝒖𝒆𝒔𝒕𝒆𝒅: not too full/dense but still noticeable, wants to elongate her eyes but without the ends drooping

𝑪𝒍𝒊𝒆𝒏𝒕'𝒔 𝒆𝒚𝒆 𝒔𝒉𝒂𝒑𝒆: almond, wide-set

𝑾𝒉𝒂𝒕 𝒘𝒆 𝒅𝒊𝒅: wispy wet set, kitten-eye shape (no droopy ends here🙅🏻‍♀️), 9-13mm base, 10-15mm spikes, CC curl 

I always do an in-depth consultation especially with my new clients so that their set is fully customised to suit their eye shape. This also allows me to understand my client’s preferences so that we can work out something that they’ll love!!

**Caption**:
𝚌𝚕𝚊𝚜𝚜𝚒𝚌 ☁️

can you believe this is a classic set 🫢 her natural lashes were so full so this set turned out amazing😮‍💨🤌 a reminder that since classic sets are a 1:1 natural lash to extension application, the set will only turn out as full as your natural lashes! that means if u have gaps or sparse natural lashes, this set will probably not be suitable for you and it might be better to opt for a hybrid set instead 🤗🤗
